#### Summary:
```python
"""
Automatically generated alpha features from Phase 2 Discovery.
DO NOT EDIT MANUALLY. USE AlphaPromoter TO UPDATE.
"""
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np

def compute_promoted_alphas(panel: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Compute all promoted alpha features on the input panel."""
    # Panel must be MultiIndex [timestamp, symbol]
    out = pd.DataFrame(index=panel.index)

    # Step 1: Create lagged environment to prevent leakage
    symbol_level = panel.index.names[1]
    lagged = panel.groupby(level=symbol_level).shift(1)

```
#### Imports:
- import pandas as pd
- import numpy as np

```python
"""
Automatically generated alpha features from Phase 2 Discovery.
DO NOT EDIT MANUALLY. USE AlphaPromoter TO UPDATE.
"""
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np

def compute_promoted_alphas(panel: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Compute all promoted alpha features on the input panel."""
    # Panel must be MultiIndex [timestamp, symbol]
    out = pd.DataFrame(index=panel.index)

    # Step 1: Create lagged environment to prevent leakage
    symbol_level = panel.index.names[1]
    lagged = panel.groupby(level=symbol_level).shift(1)

```

```python
"""
Advanced Circuit Breaker V14 ELITE - Phase 4.1 + P0-P3 EMERGENCY PATCH
Layer 04: Risk Management

STRICT LIMITS for institutional-grade risk control:
- VaR (95%) = 2.5% (down from 3%)
- CVaR (95%) = 4.5% (P1 PATCH: adjusted from 4.0%)
- Correlation Hard Stop = 0.75
- ADV Limit = 40% hard cap for all assets
- DE-RISKING: If any breach, reduce total portfolio exposure by 20% immediately
- LIMIT-LOCK: Detect HOSE/HNX floor-lock (zero bids) and block further exit orders
- BOARD-LOT: All orders must be multiples of 100 shares (P1 PATCH)

Key Features:
- PIT-compliant (no lookahead bias) - P0 PATCH: strict T-1 enforcement
```

### FILE: archive\consolidation_l2\pit_guardian_clean.py
#### Summary:
```python
"""
PIT Guardian - Point-in-Time Safety Precondition (P0-1)

This module enforces 3-Timestamp PIT compliance BEFORE any pipeline execution:
- Feature Timestamp: When features were computed
- Decision Timestamp: When trading decision was made
- Execution Timestamp: When order was placed

All timestamps must satisfy: Feature < Decision <= Execution
This prevents look-ahead bias and ensures institutional-grade data integrity.

Usage:
    guardian = PITGuardian()
    assert guardian.verify_pit_compliance(feature_ts, decision_ts, execution_ts), "PIT violation"
"""

```

- from dataclasses import dataclass
- from datetime import datetime, timedelta
- from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any

### FILE: archive\layers_08-13_deprecated\intelligence\data_quality.py
#### Summary:
```python
from typing import List, Dict, Any

class DataQualityChecker:
    """
    Kiểm tra chất lượng dữ liệu đầu vào.
    Bỏ qua xử lý chia tách (vì data sàn đã adjusted).
    Tập trung vào phát hiện lỗi: NaN, Outliers, Gaps.
    """
    def __init__(self, max_missing_pct: float = 0.05, price_jump_threshold: float = 0.15):
        # Mặc định price jump 15% (UPCOM max biên độ 15%), nếu vượt quá trong 1 phiên có thể là lỗi data
        self.max_missing_pct = max_missing_pct
        self.price_jump_threshold = price_jump_threshold

    def check_missing_values(self, values: List[float]) -> bool:
        """Trả về True nếu tỷ lệ missing values <= ngưỡng cho phép."""

```

```python
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
```

```python
ORDER: tuple[Mode, ...] = ("discover", "build", "review", "test", "audit", "patch", "done")
```

```python
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import replace
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Callable
from .dag import load_dag, topo_sort
from .types import HybridState, ReportBundle, StepResult

def next_phase(phase: str, reports: ReportBundle) -> StepResult:
    if phase == "discover":
        return StepResult("build" if reports.discovered else "discover", reports.discovered, "discovery complete" if reports.discovered else "awaiting discovery")
    if phase == "build":
        return StepResult("review" if reports.built else "build", reports.built, "build complete" if reports.built else "awaiting build")
    if phase == "review":
        ok = reports.reviewed and not reports.blocking_errors
        return StepResult("test" if ok else "review", ok, "review passed" if ok else "awaiting review")

```

```python
    def decide(self, current_metric: float, baseline_metric: float, drawdown: float | None = None, sharpe: float | None = None) -> str:
```

```python
import json
from pathlib import Path

audit_path = Path('state/audit_system.jsonl')
if audit_path.exists():
    with open(audit_path, 'r') as f:
        logs = [json.loads(line) for line in f]
    
    # Filter for rebalancer events
    rebalancer_logs = [log for log in logs if 'rebalance' in log.get('event', '').lower()]
    print(f'Total rebalancer logs: {len(rebalancer_logs)}')
    if rebalancer_logs:
        print('\nFirst 10 rebalancer events:')
        for log in rebalancer_logs[:10]:
            print(f"  {log.get('event')}: {log.get('data')}")
```

```python
"""
Automatically generated alpha features from Phase 2 Discovery.
DO NOT EDIT MANUALLY. USE AlphaPromoter TO UPDATE.
"""
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np

def compute_promoted_alphas(panel: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Compute all promoted alpha features on the input panel."""
    # Panel must be MultiIndex [timestamp, symbol]
    out = pd.DataFrame(index=panel.index)

    # Step 1: Create lagged environment to prevent leakage
    symbol_level = panel.index.names[1]
    lagged = panel.groupby(level=symbol_level).shift(1)

```
#### Imports:
- import pandas as pd
- import numpy as np

```python
"""
V21 Runtime Guard - AST Guard Module stub
"""

class ASTGuard:
    def __init__(self):
        self.rules = []
    
    def validate(self, code_str: str) -> bool:
        """Validate AST structure."""
        if not code_str or not isinstance(code_str, str):
            return False
        # Stub: basic check
        return len(code_str.strip()) > 0
```

### FILE: src\v21_system\intelligence\data_quality.py
#### Summary:
```python
from typing import List, Dict, Any

class DataQualityChecker:
    """
    Kiểm tra chất lượng dữ liệu đầu vào.
    Bỏ qua xử lý chia tách (vì data sàn đã adjusted).
    Tập trung vào phát hiện lỗi: NaN, Outliers, Gaps.
    """
    def __init__(self, max_missing_pct: float = 0.05, price_jump_threshold: float = 0.15):
        # Mặc định price jump 15% (UPCOM max biên độ 15%), nếu vượt quá trong 1 phiên có thể là lỗi data
        self.max_missing_pct = max_missing_pct
        self.price_jump_threshold = price_jump_threshold

    def check_missing_values(self, values: List[float]) -> bool:
        """Trả về True nếu tỷ lệ missing values <= ngưỡng cho phép."""

```

- from typing import List, Dict, Any

#### Summary:
```python
"""
Black-Litterman Model for Portfolio Optimization (v2 - v21.24.0).

Implements the Black-Litterman framework which combines:
1. Market equilibrium returns (from market cap weights)
2. Investor alpha views (explicit forecasts)
3. Uncertainty in views (confidence levels)

v2 Upgrades:
- L2 Regularization: Prevents extreme weight concentrations
- Liquidity Constraints: Max weight <= 10% of ADV (Average Daily Volume)
- Regime-Aware Confidence: Adjusts view confidence based on HMM regime_confidence

Produces posterior expected returns that are more stable than pure alpha views.
"""
```

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from statistics import mean


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PortfolioOptimizer:
    risk_parity_floor: float = 0.0

    def normalize(self, weights: dict[str, float]) -> dict[str, float]:
        total = sum(abs(v) for v in weights.values()) or 1.0
        return {k: v / total for k, v in weights.items()}

    def covariance_matrix(self, returns_by_asset: dict[str, list[float]]) -> dict[str, dict[str, float]]:
```

```python
- Execution Timestamp: When order was placed

    guardian = PITGuardian()
    assert guardian.verify_all_data(feature_ts, decision_ts, execution_ts), "PIT violation"
```

### FILE: src\v21_system\layer_08_situational_awareness\cii_module.py
#### Summary:
```python
"""
Country Intelligence Index (CII) Module - V21_QUANT Layer 08
Adapted from koala73/worldmonitor country-instability.ts

INSTITUTIONAL GRADE IMPLEMENTATION:
- Vectorized operations (NumPy)
- Point-in-time (PIT) safe: all timestamps explicit
- No look-ahead bias
- Clear data lineage
- Auditable scoring logic

CII Methodology (from WorldMonitor):
- 4 Component Scores: Unrest (25%), Conflict (30%), Security (20%), Information (25%)
- Contextual Boosts: Hotspot activity, news urgency, focal points
```

#### Summary:
```python
"""
Risk Engine – V21_QUANT

This module defines the core risk parameters used throughout the pipeline. Adjustments have been made to align with the current capital (2 B VND) and to resolve the 0% return issue observed in backtest (P1-01).
"""

class RiskEngine:
    def __init__(self):
        # Board lot for VN securities (minimum 100 shares per trade)
        self.vn_board_lot = 100  # unchanged – correct value for VN market
        # ADV (Average Daily Volume) unit – switched from 'shares' to 'vnd' to match capital units
        self.adv_unit = 'vnd'  # <-- changed from 'shares' to 'vnd'
        # ADV limit as a fraction of capital – loosened to allow larger positions
        self.adv_limit_pct = 0.5  # <-- loosened from 1.0 to 0.5 (50% of capital)
        # Additional risk parameters can be added here as needed

```

```python
import importlib

def test_import_and_metadata():
    mod = importlib.import_module("v19_system.layer_02_type_spec.schema_registry_module")
    info = mod.module_info()
    assert info["layer_id"] == "layer_02_type_spec"
    assert info["module_id"] == "schema_registry_module"
    assert isinstance(info["node_ids"], list)
    assert len(info["node_ids"]) >= 1
    assert hasattr(mod, 'schema_registry_module_validate_input')
    assert hasattr(mod, 'schema_registry_module_core_logic')
    assert hasattr(mod, 'schema_registry_module_post_process')
    assert hasattr(mod, 'schema_registry_module_failure_route')

def test_run_is_stub_until_implemented():
```

```python
from v19_system.autonomy import AutonomousState, next_state, run_closed_loop

def test_next_state_advances():
    s = AutonomousState(mode="discover", round_id=0)
    s2 = next_state(s)
    assert s2.mode == "build"
    assert s2.round_id == 1

def test_run_closed_loop_completes_when_facts_present():
    state = run_closed_loop({"tree_indexed": True, "dag_loaded": True, "spec_loaded": True, "build_done": True, "review_done": True, "tests_passed": True, "audit_clean": True})
    assert state.mode == "done"
```

#### Summary:
```python
from v19_system.types import HybridState, ReportBundle
from v19_system.runner import next_phase
from v19_system.orchestrator import autonomous_orchestrate
from v19_system.dag import load_dag, topo_sort

def test_phase_progression():
    r = ReportBundle(discovered=True)
    s = HybridState(phase="discover", round_id=0, reports=r)
    out = next_phase(s.phase, s.reports)
    assert out.next_phase == "build"

def test_autonomous_orchestrate_reaches_done():
    final = autonomous_orchestrate("graphs/global_execution_dag.yaml")
    assert final.phase == "done"


```

### FILE: tests_v20\test_v20_quant_core.py
#### Summary:
```python
from v20_system.types import PipelineFrame
from v20_system.temporal import TemporalFirewall
from v20_system.risk import RiskEngine
from v20_system.portfolio import PortfolioOptimizer
from v20_system.orderbook import OrderBookLevel, OrderBookSimulator
from v20_system.execution import ExecutionEngine
from v20_system.state import StateEngine
from v20_system.feedback import FeedbackLoop
from v20_system.pipeline import UnifiedQuantPipeline

def test_temporal_firewall_blocks_overlap():
    fw = TemporalFirewall()
    f = PipelineFrame(timestamps=[1,2,3], values={"x":[1,2,3]})
    t = PipelineFrame(timestamps=[3,4,5], values={"y":[1,2,3]})
    assert fw.validate(f, t).ok is False

```

```python
import math
import pytest
from v21_system.layer_01_core_intelligence.advanced_features import (
    compute_rsi, compute_macd, compute_bollinger_bands,
    compute_atr, compute_money_flow, compute_adx
)
from v21_system.layer_01_core_intelligence.feature_module import FeatureModule, FeatureModuleConfig

def test_compute_rsi():
    closes = [10.0, 11.0, 12.0, 11.5, 12.5, 13.0, 14.0, 13.5, 14.5, 15.0, 16.0, 15.5, 16.5, 17.0, 18.0]
    rsi = compute_rsi(closes, period=14)
    assert len(rsi) == len(closes)
    assert math.isnan(rsi[0])  # Warmup period
    assert not math.isnan(rsi[-1])
    assert 0 <= rsi[-1] <= 100
```

### FILE: tests_v21\test_layer01_intelligence.py
#### Summary:
```python
import pytest
from v21_system.intelligence.data_quality import DataQualityChecker
from v21_system.intelligence.data_ingestion import DataIngestionEngine, MarketData

def test_data_quality_missing():
    checker = DataQualityChecker(max_missing_pct=0.20)
    # 1 missing out of 4 = 25% > 20% -> Fail
    assert checker.check_missing_values([1.0, 2.0, None, 4.0]) is False
    # 1 missing out of 5 = 20% <= 20% -> Pass
    assert checker.check_missing_values([1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0, None]) is True
    # Empty list -> Fail
    assert checker.check_missing_values([]) is False

def test_data_quality_outlier():
    checker = DataQualityChecker(price_jump_threshold=0.15)

```

```python
def test_signal_blender_rank_normalization():
    blender = SignalBlender(normalization="rank")
    model_scores = {
        "m1": {"AAA": 3.0, "BBB": 1.0, "CCC": 2.0},
        "m2": {"AAA": 1.0, "BBB": 2.0, "CCC": 3.0},
    }
    weights = {"m1": 0.5, "m2": 0.5}

    out = blender.blend(model_scores, weights)
    assert set(out.final_scores.keys()) == {"AAA", "BBB", "CCC"}
```

### FILE: tests_v21\test_risk_limits.py
#### Summary:
```python
import pytest
from v21_system.layer_03_execution_engine.risk_limits import RiskGuard, RiskLimitsConfig


def test_risk_guard_pass():
    """Test order passing all risk limits."""
    config = RiskLimitsConfig(max_notional_value_per_trade=1_000_000_000, max_adv_percentage=0.1)
    guard = RiskGuard(config)
    
    order = {"symbol": "VNM", "quantity": 1000, "side": "BUY"}
    clamped_order = guard.check_and_clamp(order, current_price=100000, adv=1000000, adv_unit="shares")
    
    # 1000 * 100000 = 100_000_000 <= 1_000_000_000
    # 1000 <= 1000000 * 0.1 = 100000
    assert clamped_order == order

```

```python
"""Tests for V21 TargetModule."""

import pytest
from v21_system.layer_01_core_intelligence.target_module import TargetModule


def make_clean_df(symbols=("AAA",), n=20):
    timestamps = list(range(1, n + 1))
    data = {}
    for s in symbols:
        data[s] = {}
        for t in timestamps:
            data[s][t] = {"close": 100.0 + t, "volume": 1000.0}
    return {
        "index": {"symbol": list(symbols), "timestamp": timestamps},
```

#### Summary:
```python
import numpy as np
import pytest
from v21_system.layer_03_execution_engine.tca import TCA, TCALogEntry


def test_tca_is_calculation():
    """Test Implementation Shortfall (IS) calculation."""
    tca = TCA()

    # Buy order, fill price > benchmark => positive IS
    is_buy = tca.calculate_is(fill_price=101.0, benchmark_price=100.0, side="BUY", quantity=100)
    assert is_buy == 100.0  # (101-100)/100 * 10000 = 100 bps

    # Sell order, fill price < benchmark => positive IS
    is_sell = tca.calculate_is(fill_price=99.0, benchmark_price=100.0, side="SELL", quantity=100)

```
